Virtual Patent Marking
This information is provided to satisfy the virtual patent marking provisions of the America Invents Act, as provided in 35 U.S.C. § 287(a). The information is also provided to satisfy requirements for patent marking and patent information in other countries and regions.
Hence, this page serves as notice that the products listed below are covered by one or more patents that are either owned by, or licensed to, Inter IKEA Systems B.V. or a company belonging to the same group of companies. The listing may not be all inclusive. The IKEA products listed below may be protected by additional patents, including corresponding patents in other countries, and further patents may be pending. Furthermore, products that are not listed below may also be protected by one or more patents and in one or more countries.
The listing below identifies patents from several different jurisdictions/countries. Each jurisdiction or country is identified using the World Intellectual Property Organization (WIPO) Recommended Standard on Two-Letter Codes for the Representation of States, Other Entities and Intergovernmental Organizations (WIPO Standards – ST.3). For example, “US” would refer to a United States of America Patent.
IKEA products are identified below by their respective unique Article Number (as provided on at least one of the Product Label, the Product Packaging and the Assembly Instruction), the Article Number being formed by eight digits that may be grouped or may be presented in sequence in a format exemplified by: 30332116, 303.321.16, or 303-321-16. For each Article Number the associated patent numbers are listed. These IKEA products are covered by or for use under one or more of the following patents as listed below.
For your information: This patent information was last updated on the 24th of October 2025 and will continue to be updated periodically.
